[p. 001]

ṛjuvimalā pañcikā
śālikanāthamiśrakṛtā

(bṛhatīvyākhyā)
sṛ1ṣṭāvidyāniśādhvaṃsinibandhamayavāsaram |

ujjāsitajagajjāḍyaṃ namasyāmaḥ prabhākaram || 1 ||

prabhākaramayīṃ dṛṣṭiṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadhataṃ sadā |

vāmadarśanatāpannacandraṃ vande’parājitam || 2 ||

prabhākaragurorbhāvama2tigambhīrabhāṣiṇaḥ |

añjasā vyañjayiṣyantī pañcikā3 kriyate mayā || 3 ||

upanyāsanirāsābhyāṃ vyāsenaiṣā vidūṣitā |

vyākhyā prācāṃ nibandhṝṇāmiti nāhamadūduṣam || 4 ||

loka ityādibhāṣyaṃ yatnagauravaṃ prasajyetetyevamantamathaśabdadūṣaṇārthamaucityā-
nubhāṣaṇaparamiti vārtikakāreṇa vyākhyātam | tattu mandaprayojanamiti matvā ṭīkākāraḥ
prayojanaṃ darśayati---loka ityādibhāṣyasyetyādinā
 | sarvasūtrasthasarvapadaviṣayaṃ
1. sṛṣṭvājñānaniśā- ga. nyāyakośārambhe
dṛśyamānaḥ ślokapāṭho’trādṛtaḥ | “ujjāsita”

ityatra kvacit “ujjhāsita”
 iti dṛśyate;
sa prāmādika iti bhāti |
2. bhāvaṃ mita- ga.
3. pañjikā. ga.

cedamucyate; tena bahuvacanāvirodhaḥ | prakṛ1tatvādvipratipannatvāccāthātaḥśabdayo-

ityākṛtivādaḥ

“atha sambandhaḥka iti ? yacchabde vijñāte satyartho vijñāyate”

 iti pūrvamuktaḥ, sa
eva pṛṣṭaścopanyastaśceti | idānīṃ yenābhiprāyeṇa bhedaṃ matvā praśna uktaḥ,
tamāviṣkaroti---nanu cāsau saṃjñāsaṃjñilakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ

uktaḥ | śa2bde hyuccarite’rtho vijñāyata itīdameva śabdasya jñāpakatvamarthasya
jñāpyatvam, na punaranyat | tatra punaḥ “pratyāyyasya pratyāyakasya”
 iti ṣaṣṭhyā

2. Omitted śabde...ukta iti. ga.

pratyāyyapratyāyakabhāvātirekī saṃjñāsaṃjñilakṣaṇaḥ sambandha ukta iti | tadayuktam;

“na, siddhavadupadeśāt”

yadi vṛddhavyavahāra eva vyutpannāḥ kecana śabdā vyutpādyānāṃ nāṅgī-

vṛddhavyavahāraprasiddhāḥ kecana vyutpādyānāmaṅgīkriyante tadā kimaparaṃ sambandha-
karaṇena ? vṛddhavyavahāra eva vyutpattyupāyo’stviti | saṃprati sthite rāddhānte
codayati---nanu vyutpattirapi kāryatāmapekṣata eva
 | kathamiti praśnaḥ |
uttaram---‘ayamasya vācakaḥ’
 ityevamavijñātārthasya na śabdā1darthāvagati-
rupapadyata iti
 | etacca svapakṣatulyatāpādānārthaṃ codyam | parihārastu---na hi
dṛṣṭe’nupapannaṃ nāma | vyavahāramātrādeva hi bālānāmavagatirdṛṣṭā
 | upasaṃharati
---tasmātpratyakṣadṛṣṭe śabdārthāvagatikāraṇe vyavahārākhya evamupapadyamāne na
kartuḥ kalpanopapadyata iti pūrvameva vyākhyātam |

“arthe’nupalabdhe”
 ityasyārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe---idamaparaṃ vyavahārānaupayikaṃ
kṛtake sambandhe yadutādṛśyamānasyārthasya de2vatādeḥ saṃjñākaraṇam

1. Omitted. artha. ga.
2. devadattādeḥ. ka.

[p. 198]

asyārthaḥ---vyavahārārthaṃ saṃjñā kāryā, yathā putrasya pitrā ‘caitraḥ’
 iti | na ca
devatābhirvyavahāro’sti | nanu kimiti prayojanābhāvamātraṃ bhāṣyoktamuktam ?
‘devatādīnāṃ ca pratyetumaśakteḥ teṣu ca sambandhakaraṇamaśakyam’
 ityapi bhāṣya-
kāreṇoktam; tatkimiti na vyākhyāyata ityatrāha---aśaktistūpavarṇitaiva
 |
bhāṣye | tena na vyākhyāyate | tena “saṃjñākarmatvamasmadviśiṣṭānāṃ liṅgam”
 iti
nirākṛtam, devatādisaṃjñākaraṇasyaivāsiddheḥ | tasmādautpattikatvātsambandhasya
siddhamanapekṣatvamiti

|| iti sambandhaparihāraḥ ||

atra bhāṣyakāraḥ “atha yaduktamanimittaṃ śabdaḥ”
 ityanena bhāṣyeṇaivaṃ
codayati---atha mīmāṃsaka ! yaduktaṃ pūrvam ‘animittaṃ śabdaḥ’
 ityasmābhiḥ,
tasya kaḥ parihāraḥ iti | tadākṣipati “atha yaduktamanimittaṃ
śabdaḥ”
 iti kimidaṃ codyate
 ? yāvatā parihṛtamevaitadanimittatvaṃ
sambandhasyautpattikatvābhidhānena | athocyate---autpattikasambandhābhidhānenānyadeva
parihṛtam; atrāhaṃ---kasya vānyasya parihāro’vagamyate autpattikatvābhi
-

[p. 199]

dhānena
 ? kimiti ? naivānyadupanyastam
, yatparihriyeta | vidyamānopalambhana-
tayaiva hi viṣayāntarābhāvo niyogasya darśitaḥ “citrayā yajeta paśu-
kāmaḥ”
 ityādau
 | asyārthaḥ---vidyamānopalambhanatvāt bhavatyeveṣṭeḥ paśūnāṃ
ca sādhyasādhanabhāvasya pramāṇāntaragrāhyatā; ataḥ pramāṇāntaragamyameva
sādhyasādhanapūrvakaṃ kāryatvaṃ niyogasya liṅāderviṣayaḥ, na viṣayāntaramiti pūrvaṃ darśi-
tam | atastasyaivautpattikatvena parihāro dattaḥ | kimaparamaparihṛtamivopanyasyate ?
atra samādhiḥ---satyaṃ tasyaivautpattikatvaparihāra uktaḥ; kintvautpattike’pi
sambandhe viṣayāntaraviveko’nukta iva pratibhāti; tadvivekāyedaṃ puna-
rupanyasyate
 | asyārthaḥ---pauruṣeye hi sambandhe sati balādāpadyamānaṃ vedānāṃ
pauruṣeyatvaṃ sāpekṣatvāpādakamautpattikasambandhābhidhānena nirastam | loke tu
svātantryādarśanena śabdasya yā pramāṇāntaragocaraikaviṣayatvaśaṅkā, sā
nirākriyate | autpattike’pi sambandhe viṣayāntaraṃ svatantraṃ śabdasyetyayaṃ
viveko’nukta iva pratibhāti, tadvivekāyedaṃ codyaṃ punarupanyasyate | “karma-
kāle phalādarśanātkālāntare ca karmābhāvāt”
 ityanena bhāṣyaṃ paṭhitvākṣi-

[p. 200]

pati | nanu cānena viṣayāntaravivekāyopanyastena bhāṣyeṇa kiṃ prayojanam ?
yasmāt viṣayāntarābhāvāt pramāṇāntaraviṣayatayā ca sāpekṣatvāttadabhāve
cāprāmāṇyaṃ darśitam
 | śabdasya pramāṇāntarāvedyaviṣayatvābhāvātpramāṇāntara-
grāhyarthaviṣayatayā ca pramāṇāntarasāpekṣatvātpramāṇāntarābhāve cāprāmāṇyaṃ
darśitaṃ citrākṣepe, ihāpi tadevānena bhāṣyeṇoktamiti | kiṃ punastat
yadanuktamiva pratibhāti
 ? yadanena bhāṣyeṇocyate | samādhatte---idaṃ tat;
pramāṇāntaraviṣayatve hyanenedamityasmin sati niyogaḥ | tatra cānupalabdhe
sphuṭamevāprāmāṇyam
 iti prāguktameva | na cāsminnarthe saṃbhavati niyogādeva
niyogasiddhiriti śakyate kalpayitum
 | idaṃ tadanuktamuktamanena
bhāṣyeṇeti sambandhaḥ asyārthaḥ---yadyapi karmānantaraṃ phalaṃ na sidhyati,
kālāntare ca nāsti karma, tathāpi niyogādeva kālāntare phalaṃ bhaviṣyatīti;
tadanena parihriyate | yasmādasminnarthe pramāṇāntaraviṣayātmake saṃbhavati
niyogādeva phalasiddhiriti na śakyate kalpayitum, niyogasya tadānīmapramāṇa-
katvāt; niyogo hi pramāṇāntarāvedyasvarūpaḥ; kathaṃ pramāṇāntarasāpekṣaśabda-
prameyaḥ syāt ? tasmāt karmānantaraṃ phalādarśanāt kālāntare ca
karmābhāvānnāsti pramāṇaṃ paśuphaleṣṭiriti | tasmādanupalabdhe sphuṭamaprāmāṇyaṃ
codanāyā iti | tadidānīṃ nirākriyate---syādetadevaṃ yadyautpattikatā
na syāt; sādhitā cāsau
 | yadyautpattikaḥ sambandhaḥ, tataḥ kim ? idaṃ

[p. 201]

tataḥ
 | nānena karmaṇedaṃ phalaṃ sidhyatītyamumarthamapekṣate niyogaḥ | asyā-
bhiprāyaḥ---yadi hi pauruṣeyaḥ sambandhaḥ syāt, tadā balātpauruṣeyatā
codanāyā āpadyeta; tathā ca sati pramāṇāntaraviṣayatvāpatteḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvā-
pekṣa eva niyogārthaḥ syāt; sādhite tvapauruṣeye sambandhe svātantryāvirodhā-
nnāprāmāṇyaṃ śakyate vaditum | yattūktam---loke svātantryaṃ na pratīyata
iti, tatrāha---pramāṇāntarasamadhigamye’pi hi laukike’rthe naiva niyogasya
sādhyasādhanasidhyapekṣāvasīyate
 | asyārthaḥ---pravṛttidarśanena liṅādīnāṃ
kāryārthapratipādakatvaṃ tāvadavagamyate loke; tathā vyutpannāste svārthābhidhāne
na pramāṇāntaramapekṣante | ato yadyapi vaktā pramāṇāntarapratītāmeva kāryatāṃ
vivakṣati, tathāpi śabdaḥ svasāmarthyenaiva tāṃ pratipādayati; tena vede’pi
na sādhyasādhanāvagamapūrvako liṅādibhirartho’bhidhīyate | niyogaśabdena cātra
liṅādirabhipretaḥ | darśitaṃ ca bhāṣye---“ācāryacoditaḥ karomīti hi
dṛśyate”
, na anenāsya siddhiriti | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yataḥ pratipattā
kāryameva pratipadyānutiṣṭhati, na punaḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvam; ataḥ kurvāṇa
āpṛṣṭaḥ ‘ācāryacodito’hamācāryeṇa kāryaṃ bodhayitvā preritaḥ karomi’

iti vyapadiśati, na sādhyasādhanabhāvāgavamam | yadi pauruṣeye’pi vākye
pratipattā nirapekṣameva kāryaṃ budhyate, tadā kimiti tarhi puruṣāpekṣā sambandhasya
nirākriyate ? bhavatu pauruṣeyaḥ sambandho vedāśca pauruṣeyāḥ, tathāpi nirapekṣārthaprati-
pādakatayā prāmāṇyaṃ bhaviṣyati | atrottaram---kāraṇadoṣasambhāvanānirākaraṇāya
nirākriyate, na punarātmasaṃvittau
 | asyārthaḥ---yadi pauruṣeyatvaṃ na nirākriyeta,
tadotpannāpi kāryapratītiḥ kāraṇadoṣānmithyā syāt; pramāṇapratītaṃ hi svayamarthaṃ
puruṣaḥ parasmai pratipādayati | tatra ca pauruṣeyasya vacanasya kāryasya kāraṇā-

[p. 202]

numāpakatā | yadi ca vedānāṃ pauruṣeyatā syāt, tadā pramāṇāntareṇa vedārthaṃ

dutpannāpi pratītirmithyā syāt; ‘caityaṃ vandeta svargakāmaḥ’
 ‘saṅghāya bhaktaṃ dadyāt
svargakāmaḥ’
 itivat; na punarātmasaṃvittau pramātuḥ svayaṃ pratipattau sāpekṣatā

nena vivekaḥ pradarśyate
 | yadi svātantryaṃ niyogasya, na1 tarhi svargayāgayoḥ sādhya-

tasmātkāryaphalasiddhiścātaḥ’
 ityevaṃ kriyāśaktiṃ nirūpya sādhyasiddhiḥ | kiṃ tarhi ?

viṣayāpekṣitā viṣaye
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyabhāvaḥ pramāṇaṃ tadā pratyakṣādi-
paricchinnātiriktaviṣayāntarānupapatteḥ sāpekṣatā abhāvasya syāt | tathā hi---
na tāvat pratyakṣeṇa bhūtalaṃ ghāṭādisadbhāve sātireki svarūpameva gabhyaṃ ‘nāsti’

iti pratyetavyam; kintu dṛśye’pi ghaṭādau svarūpamātreṇopalabdhamiti; tena
sāpekṣatā viṣaye paricchedye’bhāvasya tadatiriktaviṣayāntarābhavāt | yacca
svātantryeṇa viṣayaṃ nopasthāpayati, na tatpramāṇāntaram | ataḥ pramāṇāntara-
pramitaviṣayāvacchedakatayaivāsya lakṣaṇābhidhānam
 | pramāṇānāṃ pratyakṣādīnāṃ
viṣayasyāvacchedakatayā ‘ayameva’
 ityevaṃ niścāyakatayāsya lakṣaṇam, na punaḥ
pramāṇāntaratayā | anena lakṣaṇagranthavirodhaḥ parihṛtaḥ | saṃpratyākṣepaṃ pariharati---
vyavahārānyatvaṃ tu saṃpratipannameva
 | nāstīti vyapadeśasya niḥśaṅkagamanā-
dilakṣaṇasya vyavahārasyānyatvaṃ saṃpratipannameva saṃmatameva | vyavahārasiddhayarthaścā-
yamupanyāsaḥ, na pramāṇasvarūpasiddhayarthaḥ
 | śabdenāpi vyavahāraḥ kartavya
ityevamarthamupanyastam---syādetadevam yadi pañcaiva pramāṇānyabhaviṣyanniti |
tenāyamartho bhavati---yadi pañcaiva pramāṇavyavahārasādhanānyabhaviṣyan, yāvatā
śabdo’pi pramāṇavyavahārasādhanamiti | na punaḥ pramāṇasvarūpamevātra sādhyate,
yena pañcaśabdātpramāṇāntaramāpadyeta | nanvevaṃ kimiti vyākhyāyate ? yāvatā
pramāṇaśabdaḥ pramāṇasvarūpapara evāstu; pramāṇavyavahārasādhanābhiprāyeṇa mā
vyākhyāyi; abhāvaśca pramāṇantaramabhyupagamyatām; tadāha---pramāṇāntarābhyupagame
ko doṣa iti
 | pariharati---na khalu kaścit brahmahatyādiḥ
; kintvapramāṇaṃ

ṇam

 | ‘ayameva’
 iti pramāṇaviṣayamavacchidya sādhyavadhāraṇa-

 iti

hatajñānaṃ bhagavantaṃ kañcana munivaraṃ dharmātiśayasaṃpannaṃ dharmabalenaivāpratihata-

[p. 285]

jñānam | yathāhuḥ---“ārṣaṃ siddhadarśanaṃ ca dharmebhyaḥ”
 iti | yāvaddhayevaṃvidhaḥ
puruṣo lokairna kalpitaḥ, tāvattadvacanabhūtādvedādarthaniścayapūrvako vyavahāro
lokasya na sidhyati | kalpyatāmiti ṇyantāddvitīyo ṇic; lokaḥ kalpayaṃ-
stvayā vedavyavahārasamarthanavādinā prayujyatāmiti | atha vedārthasya sakala-
pramāṇāviṣayatvāttadvidhaṃ puruṣaṃ lokaḥ kalpayituṃ na śaknoti, tadā---āśrīyatāṃ
vā suragurordarśanam
 | saṃvyavahāramevedamadṛṣṭaprayojanarahitaṃ vedatrayābhihitamarthaṃ
prājñairlokayātrāsiddhayarthaṃ svakīyāṃ bhojanaśayanādilokayātrāṃ sādhayituṃ supratibandhaṃ
suprayuktaṃ ḍambhātmakamanupālyamānaṃ paśyāma iti | yadāhuḥ---

“agnihotraṃ trayo vedāstridaṇḍaṃ (puṇḍraṃ) bhasmaguṇṭhanam |

buddhipauruṣahīnānāṃ jīviketi bṛhaspatiḥ ||”
 iti |
idameva suragurordarśanaṃ yuktaṃ manyāmahe; na hi dṛṣṭe lokayātrā-
siddhayarthatve satyadṛṣṭakalpanā nyāyyā; tena yadidamucyate---yadyayamapramāṇabhūtaḥ
pauruṣeya eva vedaḥ, kimiti traivarṇikānāṃ mahatāṃ mahatā prayatnena
vedādhyayanadhāraṇatadarthānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇo vyavahāra iti, tadanyathāsiddhaṃ
kṛtam ; ṛjubhyo ḍambhena jīvikālābhena prayāsasyārthavattvāt |
dṛṣṭaścāyamarthaḥ prājñaiḥ ṛjava upajīvyanta iti | yathāprasiddhamāgamavādi-
nāmapi hetudarśanādaprāmāṇyam “yūpahastino dānamācaranti”
 ityeva-
mādiṣu
 | asyārthaḥ---ye’pyāgamaṃ pramāṇamicchanti, te’pi “yūpahastino
dānamācaranti”
 ityevamādiṣu ṛtviggatalobhalakṣaṇahetudarśanādaprāmāṇyamāhuḥ |

[p. 286]

tathā hi---kenacidṛtvijā lobhiṣṭhena śraddhāpradhāno yajamānaḥ kaścidyūpahastidānaṃ
kāritaḥ; taddarśanādeṣā smṛtiḥ kṛtā---ityādhārahetudarśanāt dṛḍhayorapi
smṛtyācārayoraprāmāṇyam; evamatrāpi jīvikālobhalakṣaṇahetudarśanādācārasya
na prāmāṇyamiti | athaitanneṣṭaṃ tadā kartavyo vā sapratyabhijñapratyakṣasya
śabdo viṣaya iti yatnaḥ | saṃprati samādhātāha---kathaṃ punarjvālā-
derivānumānācchakyate’nyatvamavagantum
 ? yathā jvālāderanumānādanyatvaṃ
śakyate’vagantum, tathā kathaṃ śabdasyānyatvaṃ śakyate’vagantum | ākṣeptāha
---nanu cānumānādityuktam
 | samādhātāha---kiṃ tadanumānamiti
 |
ākṣeptāha---abhivyañjakānāmanekaviṣayā śaktirdṛṣṭeti
 | ye’bhivyañjakāsteṣā-
manekaviṣayā śaktiḥ, yathā dīpasya; abhivyañjakāśca saṃyogavibhāgā
bhavata iti | samādhātāha---nanu cedamabhivyañjakaviṣayamanumānam
 |
anekaviṣayābhivyaktisādhakam | ekagranthenāha---kathamanyaviṣayamanumāna-
manyaviṣayasya śabdaviṣayasyānyatvasyāvacchittau hetuḥ ? punarapyekagranthenāha---
śrotrasya hyabhivyañjakasaṃskāraṃ niruṇaddhi
 | etadanumānam ‘śrotramabhivyañjakena
saṃskriyate’
 ityetannirākaroti | na punaḥ śabdasyānyatvamavagamayati
 |
ākṣeptāha---nanu coktam, na cetsaṃyogavibhāgāḥ śrotrasya saṃskāraṃ
śabdagrahaṇasāmarthyamādadhati, śabdasyaivotpādakāḥ prāpnuyuḥ; utpattai

[p. 287]

cānyatvamiti
 | yadi saṃyogavibhāgāḥ śrotraṃ na saṃskuryuḥ, tadā śabdotpā-
dakā eva bhaveyuḥ; utpattau cānyatvamiti | samādhātāha---kathamanyapratiṣedho’-
nyasyotpattimavagamayati
 ? śrotrasaṃskārasya pratiṣedhe kathamanyasya
śabdasyotpattirityarthaḥ | ākṣeptāha---anyathā vyāpārānupapatteḥ
 |
saṃskārakatve pratiṣiddhe yadi śabdotpādakatvaṃ na syāt, tadā saṃyoga-
vibhāgayorvyāpāra eva nopapadyate | ekadeśyāha---na cetsaṃskāraṃ kuryuḥ,
kimanyadataḥ śabdotpatteḥ kuryuḥ ? samādhātāha---kiṃ yasya saṃskārakatā
nopapadyate, tenotpādakena bhavitavyam
 ? ākṣeptāha---na cedutpādakaḥ,
nirvyāpāra eva syāt
 | bhavatu nirvyāpāra iti cettatrāha---anyārthatā ca
saṃyogavibhāgayorupalabdhā
 | tena vyāpāro’ṅgīkartavyaḥ; sa ca saṃskārakatve
pratiṣiddhe śabdotpattiviṣaya iti | samādhātāha---satyaṃ yadarthāḥ, tatra
bhavantūtpādakāḥ saṃskārakā vā;
 na punaḥ śabde, tadarthatvābhāvāt |
kimarthatā tarhi saṃyogavibhāgayorityatrāha---parapratipattyarthatā ca siddhā
 | paraḥ
śabdaṃ pratipadyatām---ityetadarthaṃ saṃyogavibhāgau vāyavīyāvārabhyete | tatrānayorutpā-
dakasaṃskārakatvayoryadiṣṭaṃ tatparikalpyatām; viṣayāntare śabde punaḥ sambandhā-
bhāvāt kimāspadaṃ kalpanāyāḥ ? na hi śabdasya saṃyogavibhāgayośca kaścit
sambandhaḥ; pratipattyā saha saṃyogavibhāgayoḥ sambandha iti yāvat | samādhātopa-
saṃharati---tasmādanyatve hetutāṃ saṃyogavibhāgayoḥ kathamiva naiyāyikā

[p. 288]

manyante
 ? ākṣeptāha---evaṃ manyante---na cecchrotre kāryamasti, śabde kāryaṃ
pratipadyāmahe
| asyārthaḥ---pratipattyarthatā tāvatsaṃyogavibhāgayoḥ; kiñcit
kurvata śca tādarthyam, nānyathā; tatra sarvaśabdopalabdhiprasaṅgena yadā śrotre
kāryaṃ nāsti, tadā śabde kāryaṃ vijñāyate; tatrāpi nābhivyaktiḥ kāryam,
sarvapuruṣāṇāṃ sannikṛṣṭaviprakṛṣṭānāmapi yugapacchabdopalabdhyāpatteḥ | tasmādutpattireva
śabde kāryamiti | samādhātāha---kiṃ kāryatastādarthyam
 ? yenaivaṃ bravīṣīti |
ākṣeptāha---omityucyate
| samādhātāha---siddhaṃ tarhi kāryaṃ śrotre;
na saṃyogavibhāgāḥ śabdārthāḥ---iti manyante śabdavidaḥ
| śabde tadgra‌/?/-
haṇānna śabdārthatā saṃyogavibhāgayoḥ | ākṣeptāha---nanu ca kāryadarśanānna
yuktaṃ vaktuṃ śabdavidām
 | saṃyogavibhāgāḥ śabdārthā na bhavantīti | yadā
hi śrotre sakalaśabdopalabdhiprasaṅgena saṃskāralabhaṇaṃ kāryaṃ nāsti, tadā
pāriśeṣyācchabda eva kāryaṃ vijñāyata iti kāryadarśanācchabdārthā eva saṃyogavibhāgā
ityarthaḥ | samādhātāha---kāryadarśanameva nāstīti bruvate, śabde tadgra/?/haṇāt
 |
‘śabde kāryaṃ vijñāyate---ityetadeva nāsti’
 iti bruvate śabdavidaḥ | kutaḥ ?
śabde tadgra/?/haṇāt | ‘sa evāyaṃ śabdaḥ’
 iti śabdasya tatpratyayāt utpattistāvanna
kāryam, utpattau satyāmanyatvasyāvaśyaṃbhāvitvāt; sa iti pratyayavirodhāt;
na ca śabde’bhivyaktiḥ saṃbhavatīti | ubhayavādisaṃmatamākṣeptāha---yadi
śabdasyotpattiṃ na kurvanti, kiṃ tarhi kurvanti saṃyogavibhāgāḥ
 ? samādhātāha
---śrotrasaṃskāraṃ kurvantīti brūmaḥ | ākṣeptāha---nanu cānyairnayanonmī-
lanādibhirindriyasaṃskārakaiḥ samānadeśavartisakalarūpādyupalambhahetubhiḥ sahāmīṣāṃ

[p. 289]

saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ śrotrasaṃskārakatvenābhyupagatānāṃ pratiniyataśabdopalambhahetutārūpa-
vilakṣaṇatā nopapadyata ityuktam | samādhātāha---anumānāt kāryābhāvaṃ
manyate bhavān; vayaṃ tu pratyakṣādutpattiṃ necchāmaḥ; pratyakṣānumānayośca
pratyakṣaṃ balīya iti siddham
 | asyārthaḥ---śrotre kāryaṃ na bhavatītyabhi-
vyañjakasāmānyānumānāt bhavāt manyate, śrotre ca kāryābhāvamanumāne-
nāvagamya śabde tadgra/?/haṇabādhanīmutpattiṃ kalpayati; vayaṃ tu sapratyabhijñāt
pratyakṣāt śabdasyaikatāṃ pratipadyamānā utpattimanicchantaḥ śrotrasaṃskāra-
mabhivyañjakasāmānyānumānaprasaktasakalopalambhabādhena niyataśabdaviṣayaṃ kalpayāmaḥ;
evaṃ ca tvadīyaṃ śabdotpattyanumānam anumānāntarapūrvakam; asmadīyaṃ
tu pratyakṣapūrvakaṃ niyataśabdaviṣayaśrotrābhivyaktikalpanam; pratyakṣānumānayośva
śīghrapravṛttatayā pratyakṣaṃ balīya iti sakalavādināṃ siddham; anumāne
tu sambandhavyāptigrahaṇasmaraṇapakṣadharmatādhyavasānalakṣaṇā sā1magrī bahutaravilamba-
saṃpā2danīyā; pratyakṣe tu nārthāntaraparigrahasavyapekṣā kā3cidastīti śīghratara-
pravṛttānumānavaśenaiva vastunirṇaya iti---yuktaṃ tadgra/?/haṇe sati niyataviṣayābhi-
vyaktijanakatvaṃ saṃyogavibhāgayoḥ | ākṣeptāha---nanu cāgrahaṇaṃ bhramaheturityuktaṃ
jvālādīnāmiva
 | asyārthaḥ---yadi tadgrahaṇamevedaṃ siddhaṃ syāt, tadā
tadbalabhāvyanumānaṃ balīyo bhavet; yāvatā yathā jvālādiṣu bhinneṣvapi
bhedāgraha4ṇamātrāttadgra/?/haṇaṃ bhramaḥ, tathā ayamapi tadgrahaṇabhrama eveti
kathametatpūrvakamanumānaṃ balīya iti | samādhātāha---iha tvagrahaṇamevāsiddham
 |
1. sāmagrībahutā. ka.
2. saṃvedanīyā. ga.
3. kadācit. ka.
4. grahaṇādbhramaḥ. ga.

[p. 290]

nātra bhedāgrahaṇametaditi niścitam; tena pratītibalāttadgra/?/ha evāyamiti
siddhametatpūrvakasyānumānasya balīyastvam | ākṣeptā sāmyāpādanābhiprāyeṇāha---
tatra jvālādau kathamagrahaṇasiddhiriti | samādhātottaramāha---kāryānumānāt
 |
prabhāvitatilakṣaṇāt kāryājjvālādiṣu bhedānumānāt bhedāgrahaṇaṃ siddham; iha tu
tadeva kāryaṃ bhedānumānahetubhūtamadyāpi na siddham | na cāsiddhaṃ śabde bhedāgrahaṇa-
siddhau paryāptaṃ bhavati | pratyakṣaṃ tvanapekṣatvāt siddham | ākṣeptāha---anumāne vā
kāpekṣā
 ? na kācidapekṣetyarthaḥ | samādhātāha---iyamevāpekṣā---siddhe’numāne
kāryam
 | nātra prabhātulyaṃ kāryaṃ pratyakṣasiddhamastītyanumānata eva kāryamavagantavyam;
ataḥ siddhe’numāne kāryaṃ vijñeyam; kārye ca satyanumānam
 | yadapi
tadanumānam, tadapi liṅgāpekṣamiti tasya kāryaṃ liṅgaṃ vaktavyam; ataḥ
kārye satyanumānena pravartitavyam; ato’numeyakāryāpekṣatvāt sāpekṣa-
manumānam | na ca tadanumeyaṃ kāryamastīti nānumānāt bhedāgrahaṇam |
asati bhedāgrahaṇe tadgra/?/haṇametaditi sidhyati | na punarevaṃ pratyakṣaṃ
tadgrahaṇāpekṣam
 | yathānumānaṃ bhedāgrahaṇāpekṣam, naivaṃ pratyakṣaṃ tadgra/?/haṇā-
pekṣam; pratyakṣādeva hi tadgra/?/haṇaṃ sivyati | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yāvaddhi
bhedāgrahaṇanibandhano’yaṃ bhrama iti pratyakṣamanyathāsiddhaṃ na kriyate, na
tāvattadvalabhāvyanumānaṃ niroddhauṃ śakyate; aniruddhe ca casmin śabdotpattyanumānaṃ
nātmānaṃ labhate; tena bhedāgrahaṇasāpekṣamanumānam, pratyakṣaṃ tu na kiñci-
dapekṣata iti | yuktyantaraṃ cāha---saṃyogavibhāgau ca śrotrārthatayā-
nyataḥ prasiddhau
| tena yadarthatayā saṃyogavibhāgau prasiddhau, tatraiva

[p. 291]

saṃskārajanakāviti yuktam | kuto’nyataḥ
 ? śrotrārthatayā prasiddhau |
uktam “darśanasya parārthatvāt”
 iti lokataḥ
 | darśanasyoccāraṇasya
parārthatvāt parapratipattyarthatvācchrotṛgataśabdapratipattyarthatvācchrotaryeva kāryaṃ nyāyyam;
tatra śrotari kāryāsaṃbhavāt tadīye śrotre kāryaṃ yuktam | tasmāt
tādarthye parapratipattyarthatve sati yathā saṃbhavati kāryam, tathā kāryaṃ
kalpanīyam; tatra śrotaryasaṃbhavācchrotra evetyuktam | na punaḥ śabdagataṃ
kāryaṃ parikalpya śabdārthatvam; anyataḥ kāryasya śabdavartino’navagamādityuktam
‘śabde tadgra/?/haṇāt’
 iti | samādhātopasaṃharati---tasmānnātrānyatve jvālādīnā-
miva pramāṇamasti | prakṛtaṃ nigamayati---tasmāt sataḥ paramadarśanamiti
yuktamanaikāntikatvam
 | ākṣeptāha---yadi śrotre kāryam, kathaṃ tarhyeka-
viṣayaivābhivyaktiḥ śakyate kalpayitum
 ? atyantānupapannametat---
yat kañcideva śabdaṃ gṛhṇāti, kañcinneti, sarvoṣāṃ sannihitatvāditi |
samādhatte---kāryadarśanāt pratiniyatatattacchabdābhivyaktilakṣaṇakāryadarśanādyukta-
manyatrādṛṣṭasya pratiniyatopalambhanasāmarthyasya kalpanam, na punardṛṣṭasya
tadgrahaṇasya śabdavartino’pahṇavo nyāyya iti | nanvevaṃ sati bahavaḥ saṃyoga-
vibhāgā aṅgīkaraṇīyā abhivyañjakāḥ, anyathā kathaṃ pratiniyamenāneka-
śabdopalambhaḥ syāt ? tadaṅgīkāre ca kalpanāgauravamityatrāha---kāryāntara-
darśanāttu kāraṇānyatvakalpanā nyāyyaiveti
 | “uparatayoḥ saṃyogavibhāyoḥ

[p. 292]

śrūyata iti cet, naitadevam; na nūnamuparamanti saṃyogavibhāgāḥ, yata
upalabhyate śabdaḥ; na hi te pratyakṣāḥ”
 iti bhāṣyamanubhāṣitam |
atra codyabhāṣye “uparatayoḥ saṃyogavibhāgayoḥ”
 iti kau tau
saṃyogavibhāgāvabhipretau
 ? yayoruparatayorapi śabda upalabhyata iti codyata
ityarthaḥ | uttaram---tālvādīnāṃ saṃyogavibhāgāvabhipretāviti brūmaḥ |
kuta etat
 ? uttaram---bhāṣye “uparatayoḥ”
 iti śravaṇāt | nanu
yadyapyevaṃ śrūyate, tathāpi kimiti vāyavīyāvapi na pratīyete ? tatrāha
---vāyavīyānāṃ tūtpattiruparatiścānumeyā
 | asyārthaḥ---yadi vāyavīyābhi-
prāyeṇedamucyate, tadā tayoḥ kāryagamyayoḥ kārye satyuparatireva na śakyate vaktum;
vakti ca; tato’vagacchāmaḥ---tālvādisaṃyogavibhāgābhiprāyeṇedamucyata iti |
tenāyaṃ codyabhāṣyārthaḥ---tālvādīnāṃ saṃyogavibhāgāvabhivyañjakau na bhavataḥ,
uparatayorapi tayoḥ śabdaśravaṇāditi | atra parihārabhāṣyam “na nūnamuparamanti
saṃyogavibhāgāḥ”
 iti | atra ko’bhiprāyaḥ ? vāyavīyān saṃyogavibhāgān
manasi kṛtvā kāryasamadhigamyatvādidamuttaram
 | kāryasamadhigamyā vāyavīyāḥ
saṃyogavibhāgāḥ kārye sati noparatā ityabhiprāyaḥ; vāyavīyābhiprāyeṇaivedamuttara-
mityetat “na hi te pratyakṣāḥ”
 ityanena bhāṣyeṇa spaṣṭīkṛtam | tālvādīnāṃ tu

[p. 293]

saṃyogavibhāgau pratyakṣāveveti tadabhiprāyeṇa ‘na hi te saṃyogavibhāgāḥ pratyakṣāḥ’

iti vacanaṃ naiva ghaṭate; ato vāyavīyābhiprāyeṇaiva parihāraḥ | ākṣipati---
yadyevaṃ spaṣṭīkṛtam, idānīmuttaragrantho nopapadyate “ākāśaviṣayatvāttasya,
ekatvāccākāśasya”

 iti | kathaṃ nopapadyate ? vāyavīyānāmabhivyañjakatve
kāryapakṣa iva ‘yatra te, tatraivopalabdhyā bhavitavyam’
 ityevaṃ nopapadyate |
asyārthaḥ---bhāṣye hi “yadi saṃyogavibhāgāḥ śabdamabhivyañjanti, na kurvanti,
tadā ākāśaviṣayatvāttasyaikatvāccākāśasya ya evāyamatra śrotrākāśāḥ sa
eva deśāntareṣvapīti srughnasthairabhivyaktaḥ pāṭaliputre’pyupalabhyeta”
 ityanena granthena
saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ śabdābhivyañjakatve ekatrābhivyaktasya sarvatropalabdhiḥ syāditi
codyaṃ kṛtam; tacca vāyavīyānāmabhivyañjakatve nopapadyate, teṣāṃ prāpyābhivyañja-
katvāt | tena yatraiva te, tatraivābhivyaktirbhaviṣyati | tālvādisaṃyogavibhāgābhi-
prāyeṇa tu codyamidamupapadyate, tayoraprāpyābhivyañjakatvāt | ākṣeptaiva
samādhāturāśaṅkāmupanyasyati---athāyamabhiprāyaḥ, abhivyaktipakṣe na vāyvā-
śritāḥ śabdā bhavanti | kiṃ tarhi ? ākāśasthā eva
 | tenaikatvāt
sarvatrākāśasya sarvatropalabhyata ityupapadyate codyamabhivyaktipakṣe | yeṣāṃ punaḥ

[p. 294]

kriyante, teṣāṃ vāyavīyāḥ saṃyogavibhāgā asamavāyikāraṇatāṃ pratipadyante
 |
yadā caite asamavāyikāraṇabhūtāḥ tadā tadeva vāyudravyaṃ samavāyikāraṇaṃ bhavati;
samavāyikāraṇapratyāsannaṃ hyasamavāyikāraṇaṃ bhavati, yathā tantuṣu samavāyi-
kāraṇabhūteṣu samavetāstantusaṃyogāḥ paṭasya tantusamevatasya, yathā ca tanturūpāṇi
tantusamavetapaṭarūpotpattau; tena vāyupratyāsannā vāyavīyāḥ saṃyogavibhāgā iti
vāyureva samavāyikāraṇam | yatra ca samavāyikāraṇam, tatraiva kāryopalabdhiḥ; yathā
yatra tantavastatraiva paṭopalabdhiriti yuktamiti | idānīmimāmāśaṅkāṃ dūṣayati---
evamapi nirjñāte vāyavīyasaṃyogavibhāgayorabhivyañjakatve codyaṃ nopapadyate | yasmāt
yatraivābhivyañjakam, tatraivābhivyaktiḥ yathā yatraiva kāraṇam, tatraiva kāryamiti

jāyate, sā kathaṃ vyutpattiśūnyaiva bhavati ? bhavati ca | a2to nānvitābhidhānamiti
bhāvaḥ | parihārastvatra satyamevametaditi | vyavahārārthatvāt prayogasya viśeṣātma
-
1. guṇānvitaḥ ga.
2. ato’nanvitā-ga.

[p. 397]

katvācca vyavahārasya anvitāvagatireva tadarthānām; nānvitārthāntarasmaraṇam,
nya1vahārānaṅgatvāt
 | asyārthaḥ---vyavahārārthaṃ śabdaḥ prayujyate; vyavahāraścānvi-
tārthaviṣayaḥ; tena prayogabalādanvitārthāvagamakatvameva padānām | yatpunaretadananvi-
tārthajñānam, tatsmaraṇam; na punaḥ śabdābhidhānajanyam, vyavahārāna2ṅgatvāt;
na hyananvitena kaścidapi vyavahāraḥ kriyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati---śabdaśravaṇānantaraṃ
yadidamanvitādarthādarthāntaraṃ padārthasvarūpamātraṃ buddhisthaṃ bhavati, sā smṛtireva,
yathā arthadarśanāt padasmṛtiḥ; tatra ca na śabdasyābhidhāna3vyāpāraḥ, tena vināpi
tadupapatteḥ; yathā artho na śabdasyābhidhātā, atha ca pratisambandhitayā saṃskāro-
dbodhakatayā śabde smṛtiṃ janayati, tathā śabdo’pyartha iti | tadatra pūrvapakṣa-
siddhāntabhāṣyayorayamarthaḥ---śrutyanantarabhāvitvādananvitārthāvagaterananvita eva padārthaḥ
śrautaḥ; sa na vākyānurodhena viśeṣe’nvite’vasthāpayitumarhatīti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
rāddhāntastu---satyamananvitaḥ prathamaṃ pratīyate, tathāpi vṛddhavyavahāravyutpattāveva
kevalena padārthena vyavahārābhāvādanvita eva padārtho padānāṃ vyutpattiḥ, ananvitā-
vagatistu smaraṇamātramiti | tadidamuktam---“yaścārthādartho na sa
codanārthaḥ”
 iti
 | abhidhānavyāpārānapekṣaṃ yadananvitārthasya smaraṇam, na sa
śabdārtha iti | nanvananvitārthaḥ smaryamāṇo yadyavivakṣitaḥ, kathaṃ tarhi
anvito vivakṣita ityatrāha---na cedānīṃ tat ananvitamarthāntarasmaraṇam
avivakṣitaṃ vyavahārānupayogitvādityetāvatā, pratīyamānasyāpi śabdāt anvitasya
1. Omitted. vyavahārānaṅgatvāt. ga.
2. navagatatvāt. ga.
3. dhānaṃ vyā-ga.

[p. 398]

vyavahāropayogino’pyavivakṣitatvaṃ śakyate vaktum | atra codayati---gamyate ca
śabdānnimittāt aśabdārthaśceti kimidam
 ? yadeva śabdādavagamyate sa śabdārtha
iti smaryamāṇasyāpi śabdārthatvameva yuktam | pariharati---śabdāntarābhidheyata1yā
smaraṇāt, smṛteścāprāmāṇyāt; ataḥ śabdanimittatāpi bhrāntairevoditā
pratyakṣādīnāmiva smaraṇīye
 | saṃprayuktādvākyātmakācchabdācchabdāntaraṃ pūrvavākyaṃ
tadabhidheyatayaiva smaraṇānna śabdādavagamyate; na hi pūrvavākyāntargatapadābhidheyatayaiva
vākyāntaragatena padenocyate | api ca śabdaḥ pramāṇabhūtaḥ kathamapramāṇabhūtāṃ
smṛtiṃ janayati ? ataḥ śabdanimittatāpi bhrāntairevoditā
 | paramārthatastu
śabdo’tra nimittaṃ na bhavati; kintu śabda śravaṇādudbuddhaḥ saṃskāra evaināṃ smṛtiṃ
janayatīti; yathā pratyakṣādīnāṃ smaraṇīye’rthe nimittatā na bhavati, tathā śabda-
syāpi | “e2vaṃ ca guṇāntarapratiṣedho na śabdārthaḥ”
 iti bhāṣyamanubhāṣya
vyācaṣṭe---kalpitanimittaparihārāt parihṛtam
 | ‘kalpanānirmitanimittaḥ
padārthaḥ’
 iti kila bauddhā manvate; teṣāṃ mate’nyāpohamātraniṣṭhā śrutiḥ |
tataśca guṇāntarapratiṣedhaḥ śuklaśabdasyārtha iti tadidaṃ nirākṛtam ‘kalpita-
1. tayaiva ga.
2. iti evaṃ sati guṇā- ga.

[p. 399]

nimittaṃ na bhavati’
 iti kalpanāgranthe | “api ca prātipadikāduccarantī
dvitīyādivibhaktiḥ”
 iti bhāṣyamanubhāṣitam | vyācaṣṭe---anvitārthameva
prātipadikaṃ smaranti
 | yato hetoḥ karaṇavācinaḥ prātipadikāttṛtīyāṃ vibhaktiṃ
vidadhati; karaṇaṃ ca kriyāsambandhi bha1vati; ataśca kriyāsambandhisvārthapratipādakaṃ
prātipadikamityuktaṃ bhavati | vibhaktayaśca parasparānvayapratipādikāḥ prayogāntarasmṛta-
syārthasya prayogāntare yaḥ pratipanno’rthaḥ smaryate tasya anvayitāṃ śrutyaivābhidadhati;
tenānvitameva śabdenābhidhīyata iti vibhaktaya evātra pramāṇam | ato’bhyuccayopapatti-
riyam
 | ananvitasyāvivakṣāyām | pratyuktamanumānaṃ kṛtakatvāditi
 | padasaṃghātasya
kṛtakatvādvedo’pi kṛtakaḥ---iti yadanumānam, tatpratyuktaṃ vṛddhavyavahārasya
nimittatopapattyā | “api ca”
 ityanena granthena pūrvoktāṃ sambandhakaraṇāśaktiṃ
darśayati---atīndriye kārye kāryānvayiṣu cārtheṣu sambandhakaraṇaśaktiṃrnāstīti ||

1. Omitted. bhavati...sambandhi- ga.

[p. 400]

nanu yadyatīndriyakāryābhidhāyino liṅādayaḥ, tadanvitasvārthābhidhāyīni
ca padāni, kathaṃ tarhi loke tadvyatirekeṇa śabdaprayoga ityatrāha---loke
punaḥ pramāṇāntarapramitaviṣayatayā yuktaiva, tatpūrvakatvācchabdavyavahārasya
 |
yadeva hi pramāṇāntareṇa pramīyate, tadeva loko vivakṣati; tenātīndriyakāryaṃ
parityajya kriyākāryatva eva śabdaṃ prayuṅkte lakṣaṇayā; apūrvakāryatā hi kriyākārya-
tvāvyabhicāriṇī; tena tatparatayā loko liṅādikaṃ prayuṅkte; padāntarāṇyapi
kāryānvitatayaiva prayujyanta iti na laukikaprayogavirodhaḥ | “tasmātpadebhya
eva padārthapratyayaḥ, padārthobhyo vākyārthapratyayaḥ”
 iti
 bhāṣyam; tadanupapannam,
“padārthā eva vākyārthaḥ”
 iti darśitatvādityāśaṅkyāha---vyākhyātamiti
 |
vyatiṣaṅgābhiprāyamiti pūrvameva vyākhyātam | upasaṃharati---tasmādupapannaṃ

[p. 401]

vṛddhavyavahāranimittamautpattikatvaṃ sambandhasya, vedavākyārthapratipattihetutvaṃ
ca
 | upapannaṃ tasya | vilambate ityādayastu śabdapratyabhijñāmātratayā pratyuktāḥ;
abhidhāne hi sa doṣaḥ
 | ‘vilambate’
 ‘pralambate’
 ityupasargatā vi-pra-śabdayoḥ, vipra
ityatra nāmatā svarūpābhede nopapadyata iti yaduktaṃ vaiyākaraṇaiḥ, tatpratyuktam; svarūpā-
bhede’pyabhidhānarūpatā bhinnaiva; tena kvacidanyārthatā na doṣaḥ | samāsādīnāṃ
tu tatra tatra parihāro’bhidhīyate
 | yadidamuktam---samāse padadvayasambandho
na bhavati rājapuruṣasya hastinaḥ ityādi, tasyāpi parihārastatra tatrābhidhīyate;
tena so’pi na doṣaḥ ||

“vedāṃścaike sannikarṣaṃ puruṣākhyāḥ”

 atra bhāṣyam “padavākyāśraya
ākṣepaḥ parihṛtaḥ”
 iti| tatreyamāśaṅkā---nanu kimidaṃ padaṃ vākyamiti ? yāvatā na
padavyatiriktaṃ vākyaṃ nāmāsti | ataḥ kathaṃ pṛthagupanyāsa ityāśaṅkyāha---vibhāgā-
bhidhānādavibhaktakāryatvācca vivekaḥ
 | vibhāgena padānāmabhidhāyakatvāt padavyava-

[p. 402]

hāraḥ; avibhaktaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ padānāmekaṃ kāryaṃ viśiṣṭaikārthapratipattiriti tannibandhana-
steṣveva padeṣu vākyavyavahāraḥ | tena kāryavaśena padavyavahāro varṇānām,
kāryavaśena ca vākyabhāvaḥ padānāmiti yukto vākyapadayorvivekaḥ | yaduktam
‘asmaraṇātkartuḥ akṛtako vedaḥ’
 iti, tadviruddhaṃ samākhyayā | kāthakamityādikā yā
samākhyā, sā kṛtakatāṃ vedasya darśayati | atra pṛcchati---kiṃ kāryanibandha-
naiva samākhyā ?
 ayamāśayaḥ---yadi kāryanibandhanaiva samākhyā syāt,
tadā samākhyayā kṛtakatvaṃ bhavet; yāvatā pravacanādapi samākhyā ghaṭata
eveti | uttaram---bāḍham
 | atiśayena karaṇanibandhanaiva samākhyā | kutaḥ ?
asādhāraṇena hi vyapadeśo bhavati
 | karaṇaṃ hyasādhāraṇam; na
hyekena kṛtasyāpareṇa karaṇaṃ saṃbhavati | sādhāraṇaṃ ca pravaktṛtvam
 |

[p. 403]

bahavo’pi  pravaktāro bhavanti | yadi pravaktṛtvena vyapadeśo na bhavati,
kathaṃ tarhi “kṛte granthe”
, “tena proktam”
 iti ca sūtradvayaṃ
bhagavataḥ ?
 uttaram---proktavacanamayuktam, “samarthānām”
 iti virodhāt
 |
“samarthānāṃ prathamādvā”
 iti sāmarthye sati taddhito bhavati | na ca ‘kaṭhena proktam’

ityasya vigrahavākyasya yo’rthaḥ, sa kāthakaśabdādavagamyate; tena sāmarthyā-
bhāvāttasminnarthe taddhito notpadyata eva; tenedaṃ vacanamayuktam | tasmāt samākhyā-
darśanāt kāryataiva vedasyāvagamyata iti ||

atra codayati---keyaṃ kāryatā nityatvācchabdānām, autpattikatvācca
sambandhasya ?
 śabdāstāvanna kriyante, nityatvāt | na ca teṣāmarthasambandho’pi
kriyate, nityatvādeva | tasmāt keyaṃ kāryatā ? pariharati---pramāṇāntarā-
pekṣitā
 | asyārthaḥ---‘pramāṇāntareṇa ayamartho’nena pratipannaḥ’
 iti yeyaṃ
pramāṇāntarāpekṣitā, sā kṛtakatā | tathā ca kṛtakatvānurūpaṃ vedavaco dṛśyate---

[p. 404]

“babaraḥ prāvāhaṇiḥ”
 ityevamādi | pravāhaṇasyāpatyaṃ kīrtyate vede; tena
prāvāhaṇijanmanaḥ prāgayaṃ grantho nāstīti jñāyate | atrocyate---syādetadevam,
yadyanyathā karaṇamantareṇa asādhāraṇavyapadeśo nopapadyate; prakarṣādapi hyasādhāraṇa-
vyapadeśo loke dṛṣṭaḥ
 | vacana eva yaḥ prakarṣaḥ so’sādhāraṇa iti
tannibandhano’pi vyapadeśo ghaṭata eva | yathā bādarāyaṇena vedasya prāmāṇyaṃ
samākhyāyate | yata eva prakarṣādapi vyapadeśo ghaṭate, ata eva sūtradvayapraṇayanaṃ
bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ
 | “kṛte granthe,”
 “tena proktam”
 iti ca |
“samarthānām”
 ityasya cāvirodhaḥ, vacanaprakarṣe’pi sāmarthyasaṃbhavāt |

[p. 405]

yaduktam “babaraḥ prāvāhaṇiḥ”
 iti
 tatrocyate---sthite prāmāṇya
guṇavādaṃ vakṣyati
 | tena nyāyena varṇanīyametadityarthaḥ | nanvetat vakṣyamāṇaṃ
kimarthamuktaṃ tatrāha---vakṣyamāṇameva ca bhāṣyakāreṇātra vyākhyātam
 |
codayati---kimartham, ‘vakṣyāmaḥ’
 iti noktaṃ ca ?
 pariharati---asambaddhato-
panyāsārtham
 | atha kathamayamunmattoktisadṛśo na bhavatīti imāmasambaddhatā-

[p. 406]

mupanyasituṃ guṇavādenātra vyākhyātam | tāmasambaddhatāṃ pariharati---na
śakyate jyotiṣṭomādiṣu vidhyuddeśeṣu kalpayituṃ tat; ekavākyatayā
ca “vanaspatayaḥ satramāsata”
 ityevamādīnām
 | asambaddhatā nāstīti
 |

[p. 407]

“api ca avigītaḥ suhṛdupadeśaḥ”
 iti bhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe---sārvajanīna-
maviparyayaṃ darśayati
 | na vedārthe kasyacidviparyayo’stītyarthaḥ | tasmā-
ccodanaiva pramāṇaṃ dharmasyeti siddhaḥ pratijñātārthaḥ
 ||

prathamasyādhyāyasya

prathamaḥ pādaḥ
